Title: Acta et decreta secundae Synodi Provinciae Ultrajectensis, in sacello ecclesiae parochialis Sanctae Gertrudis, Ultrajecti, celebratae die XIII Septembris MDCCLXIII
Creator: The second Provincial Synod of Utrecht (13 September 1763), convoked by Archbishop Petrus Joannes Meindaerts; with a Brevis historia Ecclesiae Ultrajectinae by the unnamed editor
Publisher: Ultrajecti [Utrecht], sumptibus Societatis
Publication Date: 1764
Rights: Public Domain (published 1764, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/')
Language: lat
Source: Internet Archive item bub_gb_GbxDChL5mbkC (https://archive.org/details/bub_gb_GbxDChL5mbkC), the item's own bub_gb_GbxDChL5mbkC_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: National Library of Naples; host metadata date: 1764. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: e8c5c621a1b9a68b022788118952d329f9ddc1a6ff6d6bd6a9e055cbc3a4edb7.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. National Library of Naples copy scanned by Google and contributed to the Internet Archive; good OCR of the Latin with the long s as f and printed marginal notes interleaved. Repeated "Digitized by Google" lines are left in place. Two other scans of the same edition exist (bub_gb_ELPq5NuOK5YC, OCR 0.62; bub_gb_dAv5FktMbW0C, garbled body).
Content note: The acts of the provincial council of 1763: the editor's short history of the church of Utrecht from Willibrord, the archbishop's letter of convocation, the sessions and their decrees on doctrine and discipline (among them the sacraments of order and matrimony), the subscriptions of the fathers, and the synod's letter to Pope Clement XIII.
